Part 1 (New) (64 Questions)

#1 • Government Schemes & Initiatives

Jal Jeevan Mission completed 7 years. What is the new deadline set for completing the Jal Jeevan Mission?

  • A. March 2027
  • B. December 2028
  • C. December 2027
  • D. March 2030

Answer: December 2028

Launch & Progress: Originally launched on 15 August 2019; rural tap water coverage rose from ~16.7% to ~82% (deadline extended to December 2028).

Exam Fact: Implemented by the Department of Drinking Water and Sanitation under the Ministry of Jal Shakti (Har Ghar Jal).

#2 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

At what altitude (height) is India planning to build the world's highest railway station at Taglang La (Tanglang La) in Ladakh?

  • A. 5,540 meters
  • B. 5,068 meters
  • C. 4,850 meters
  • D. 5,256 meters

Answer: 5,256 meters

Record Surpassed: Beats China's Tanggula Railway Station (5,068 meters) on the Qinghai–Tibet Railway.

Exam Fact: Located along the strategic Bhanupali–Bilaspur–Manali–Leh railway line in Ladakh.

#3 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

At which Indian port will India's first port-based green e-Methanol plant (worth ₹2,300 crore) be established?

  • A. Deendayal Port (Kandla), Gujarat
  • B. Paradip Port, Odisha
  • C. Jawaharlal Nehru Port (JNPA), Maharashtra
  • D. Visakhapatnam Port, Andhra Pradesh

Answer: Deendayal Port (Kandla), Gujarat

Project Shareholding: Total cost ₹2,300 crore; Deendayal Port Authority (DPA) holds a 76% stake in the green shipping fuel project.

Exam Fact: Deendayal Port (Kandla) is a major tidal port located in the Gulf of Kutch, Gujarat.

#4 • Government Schemes & Initiatives

Regarding Government e-Marketplace (GeM): (1) Launched on 9 August 2016; (2) Uses a 5-digit PIN code structure (14550) rather than 4 digits; (3) Celebrated its 10th Foundation Day; (4) Operates under the Ministry of Commerce and Industry. Which statements are correct?

  • A. 1 and 2 only
  • B. 1, 3, and 4 only (PIN is 5 digits: 14550)
  • C. 2 and 3 only
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1, 3, and 4 only (PIN is 5 digits: 14550)

Correction Note: Statement 2 is incorrect because the code is 5 digits (14550), not 4 digits.

Exam Fact: GeM is India's national public procurement portal operating under the Ministry of Commerce and Industry.

#5 • Science, Space & Environment

The Ministry of Earth Sciences (MoES) celebrated its 20th Foundation Day on 27 July 2026. In which year was its predecessor, the Department of Ocean Development (DOD), established?

  • A. 1988
  • B. 1972
  • C. 1981
  • D. 2006

Answer: 1981

Evolution: Department of Ocean Development was created in July 1981 and reorganized into the Ministry of Earth Sciences (MoES) in 2006.

Exam Fact: IMD, NCMRWF, IITM, and NIOT function under the Ministry of Earth Sciences.

#6 • Science, Space & Environment

Regarding Squadron Leader Bhawana Kanth: (1) Became the first woman to complete the Fighter Combat Leader (FCL) course; (2) Completed it at TACDE, Gwalior (not AFA Dundigal); (3) Commissioned in June 2016; (4) Hails from Darbhanga, Bihar (not Muzaffarpur). Which statements are correct?

  • A. 2 and 4 only
  • B. 1, 2, and 3 only
  • C. 1 and 3 only (Course at TACDE Gwalior; native of Darbhanga, Bihar)
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1 and 3 only (Course at TACDE Gwalior; native of Darbhanga, Bihar)

Corrections: She completed the FCL course at Tactics and Air Combat Development Establishment (TACDE), Gwalior, and hails from Darbhanga, Bihar.

Exam Fact: Bhawana Kanth, Avani Chaturvedi, and Mohana Singh were India's first batch of women fighter pilots commissioned in June 2016.

#7 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

On which date did the Principal Purpose Test (PPT) under the amended India–Mauritius Double Taxation Avoidance Agreement (DTAA) come into effect?

  • A. 1 April 2026
  • B. 10 August 2026
  • C. 1 July 2026
  • D. 1 October 2026

Answer: 10 August 2026

Tax Treaty Rule: Effective 10 August 2026; denies treaty tax benefits if obtaining a tax advantage was one of the principal purposes of an arrangement.

Exam Fact: Aligns the India–Mauritius DTAA with the OECD BEPS (Base Erosion and Profit Shifting) Multilateral Instrument (MLI) standards.

#8 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

Regarding Tamil Nadu's Battery Energy Storage System (BESS) Policy: (1) Targets 2.5 GW to 10 GW of BESS over 3 years; (2) New solar projects of 1 MW or more must install BESS of at least 10% capacity; (3) Storage must have a 4-hour discharge duration (not 2-hour). Which statements are correct?

  • A. 2 and 3 only
  • B. 1 and 3 only
  • C. 1 and 2 only (Discharge duration must be 4 hours)
  • D. All 1, 2, and 3

Answer: 1 and 2 only (Discharge duration must be 4 hours)

Correction Note: Statement 3 is incorrect because the policy mandates a 4-hour discharge duration (not 2 hours).

Exam Fact: Supports grid stability and peak-hour renewable energy dispatch in Tamil Nadu.

#9 • Polity, Bills & Governance

Under which Union Ministry does the Indian Cyber Crime Coordination Centre (I4C)—which launched two new portals for grievance redressal and money restoration—function?

  • A. Ministry of Home Affairs (MHA)
  • B. Ministry of Electronics and Information Technology (MeitY)
  • C. Ministry of Finance
  • D. Ministry of Communications

Answer: Ministry of Home Affairs (MHA)

Parent Ministry: Ministry of Home Affairs (headed by Union Minister Amit Shah, Lok Sabha MP from Gandhinagar, Gujarat).

Exam Fact: I4C operates the National Cyber Crime Reporting Portal (cybercrime.gov.in) and the 1930 financial cyber fraud helpline.

#10 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

Where did Hindalco commission India's first superfine Precipitated Hydrate (PHT) plant with a capacity of 30,000 tonnes per annum in August 2026?

  • A. Hirakud, Odisha
  • B. Renukoot, Uttar Pradesh
  • C. Belagavi, Karnataka
  • D. Muri, Jharkhand

Answer: Belagavi, Karnataka

Location & Capacity: Belagavi (Belgaum), Karnataka — capacity of 30,000 tonnes per annum.

Exam Fact: Hindalco Industries is the metals flagship company of the Aditya Birla Group.

#11 • Sports & Tournaments

Regarding the Khelo India & ASMITA restructuring: (1) Combined outlay for FY 2027–31 is ₹36,441 crore; (2) Khelo India component accounts for ₹29,054 crore (while ASMITA/allied accounts for ₹7,387 crore); (3) Originally launched in 2017–18 by merging 3 older schemes; (4) Created a new 'Emerging Khelo India Athletes' category. Which statements are correct?

  • A. 2 and 3 only
  • B. 1 and 2 only
  • C. 1, 3, and 4 only (Khelo India share is ₹29,054 crore)
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1, 3, and 4 only (Khelo India share is ₹29,054 crore)

Correction Note: Out of ₹36,441 crore, the Khelo India component is ₹29,054 crore and the remaining ₹7,387 crore is for the allied/ASMITA component.

Exam Fact: ASMITA stands for Achieving Sports Milestone by Inspiring Women Through Action.

#12 • Polity, Bills & Governance

Regarding Legal Metrology (Indian Standard Time) Rules, 2026: (1) IST is UTC + 5 hours 30 minutes; (2) Framed under Section 52 of Legal Metrology Act, 2009; (3) Based on 82.5° E longitude (not 75°); (4) Comes into effect after 180 days (not 30 days). Which statements are correct?

  • A. 1 and 2 only (82.5° E longitude; effective in 180 days)
  • B. 1, 2, and 3 only
  • C. 2 and 4 only
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1 and 2 only (82.5° E longitude; effective in 180 days)

Corrections: IST is anchored at 82.5° East longitude (passing through Mirzapur, UP), and the new rules take effect after 180 days (not 30 days).

Exam Fact: CSIR-National Physical Laboratory (NPL, New Delhi) is the official timekeeper of India.

#13 • Science, Space & Environment

What is the name of India's first nuclear training programme (a 3-month residential course) launched jointly by IIT Hyderabad and Crimson Energy in August 2026?

  • A. ATOM-X
  • B. ANUYAN
  • C. NUCLEAR-NEXT
  • D. PARMANU-SHIKSHA

Answer: ANUYAN

Programme Details: 3-month residential nuclear engineering & safety training programme launched by IIT Hyderabad and Crimson Energy.

Exam Fact: Prepares skilled manpower for India's Small Modular Reactor (SMR) and clean nuclear expansion.

#14 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

Regarding the National Investment and Infrastructure Fund (NIIF): (1) Total central commitment reached ₹60,000 crore after July 2026 approval; (2) Fresh funds flow into NIIF Infrastructure Fund II; (3) Founded in 2015 (not 2019); (4) Headquartered in Mumbai (not New Delhi). Which statements are correct?

  • A. All 1, 2, 3, and 4
  • B. 1, 2, and 3 only
  • C. 2 and 4 only
  • D. 1 and 2 only (Founded in 2015; HQ in Mumbai)

Answer: 1 and 2 only (Founded in 2015; HQ in Mumbai)

Corrections: NIIF was established in 2015 as India's first sovereign-linked alternative asset manager and is headquartered in Mumbai.

Exam Fact: The Government of India holds a 49% stake in NIIF, with the remainder held by domestic and international institutional investors.

#15 • Science, Space & Environment

For which client is Larsen & Toubro's Vyoma AI building India's largest NVIDIA B300 AI factory (250 MW Phase-1 capacity, 10,000 GPUs) in Chennai?

  • A. Together AI
  • B. OpenAI
  • C. Anthropic
  • D. Perplexity AI

Answer: Together AI

Location & Scale: Chennai, Tamil Nadu — 10,000 NVIDIA B300 GPUs with 250 MW Phase-1 capacity.

Exam Fact: Positions Chennai as a major sovereign and hyperscale AI data center hub in South Asia.

#16 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

Regarding India's first sovereign-backed Protection & Indemnity (P&I) marine insurance product launched in August 2026: (1) Designed by New India Assurance (NIACL); (2) Coverage is USD 1.5 billion (not USD 5 billion); (3) Operates under Bharat Maritime Insurance Pool (BMIP); (4) Covers crew, cargo, pollution, and wreck removal. Which statements are correct?

  • A. 1 and 2 only
  • B. 1, 3, and 4 only (Coverage limit is USD 1.5 billion)
  • C. 2 and 3 only
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1, 3, and 4 only (Coverage limit is USD 1.5 billion)

Correction Note: Coverage is up to USD 1.5 billion (not USD 5 billion), breaking foreign P&I club monopolies for Indian shipping.

Exam Fact: New India Assurance Company Limited (NIACL) is headquartered in Mumbai, Maharashtra.

#17 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

Under Tamil Nadu's Battery Energy Storage System (BESS) policy, what minimum percentage of capacity must new solar projects of 1 MW or more install as standalone/co-located storage?

  • A. 15%
  • B. 5%
  • C. 10%
  • D. 20%

Answer: 10%

Mandate: At least 10% of project capacity with a 4-hour discharge duration.

Exam Fact: Helps absorb midday solar surplus and supply power during evening peak demand.

#18 • Economy & Agriculture

In July 2026, NITI Aayog launched a 10-year Bioeconomy Roadmap aiming to grow India's bioeconomy to what size?

  • A. USD 691 billion
  • B. USD 300 billion
  • C. USD 500 billion
  • D. USD 1 trillion

Answer: USD 691 billion

Target: USD 691 billion over a 10-year roadmap.

Exam Fact: Complements the Union Cabinet's BioE3 (Biotechnology for Economy, Environment and Employment) Policy.

#19 • Economy & Agriculture

Which of the following is one of the three core pillars/components of the 'VISTAR' scheme approved by the Union Cabinet?

  • A. National Agricultural Census
  • B. 8th Economic Census (alongside Ecosystem & Operations and Sub-national Support)
  • C. decennial Population Census
  • D. Livestock & Fisheries Census

Answer: 8th Economic Census (alongside Ecosystem & Operations and Sub-national Support)

Three Pillars of VISTAR: (1) Ecosystem & Operations, (2) Sub-national Support, and (3) 8th Economic Census.

Exam Fact: Implemented by the Ministry of Statistics and Programme Implementation (MoSPI).

#20 • Science, Space & Environment

Regarding Centre for Development of Telematics (C-DOT): (1) Unveiled 14 indigenous quantum products on its 43rd Foundation Day; (2) Founded in 1984 (not 1994); (3) Functions under the Department of Telecommunications (DoT). Which statements are correct?

  • A. 2 and 3 only
  • B. 1 and 2 only
  • C. 1 and 3 only (C-DOT was founded in 1984)
  • D. All 1, 2, and 3

Answer: 1 and 3 only (C-DOT was founded in 1984)

Correction Note: C-DOT was established in August 1984 (not 1994) under Sam Pitroda's leadership to modernize Indian telecom.

Exam Fact: C-DOT is headquartered in New Delhi and operates under the Ministry of Communications.

#21 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

Which state received ₹150 crore financial assistance from the Ministry of Petroleum & Natural Gas in June 2026 for India's first private-sector hybrid 2G ethanol biorefinery?

  • A. Gujarat
  • B. Punjab
  • C. Maharashtra
  • D. Uttar Pradesh

Answer: Uttar Pradesh

State: Uttar Pradesh (India's largest sugarcane and ethanol-producing state).

Exam Fact: Second-generation (2G) ethanol uses agricultural crop residues (parali/bagasse) rather than food grains.

#22 • National & State Affairs

Regarding India's 80th Independence Day Celebrations (2026): (1) Theme celebrated 150 years of Vande Mataram; (2) PM Modi delivered his 13th consecutive Red Fort address; (3) Coordinated by Indian Army (not Indian Navy); (4) Har Ghar Tiranga campaign started in 2022 (not 2024). Which statements are correct?

  • A. 1, 2, and 3 only
  • B. 1 and 2 only (Coordinated by Indian Army; Har Ghar Tiranga began in 2022)
  • C. 2 and 4 only
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1 and 2 only (Coordinated by Indian Army; Har Ghar Tiranga began in 2022)

Corrections: Coordinating service was the Indian Army (not Navy), and the Har Ghar Tiranga campaign was launched in 2022 under Azadi Ka Amrit Mahotsav.

Exam Fact: 2026 marked India's 80th Independence Day (79 completed years since 15 August 1947).

#23 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

In June 2026, the Gujarat Government signed an MoU with which financial institution for ₹1 lakh crore long-term financing of metro, expressway, and Dholera projects?

  • A. SIDBI
  • B. NABARD
  • C. HUDCO (Housing and Urban Development Corporation)
  • D. NaBFID

Answer: HUDCO (Housing and Urban Development Corporation)

Institution Details: HUDCO was founded in 1970 (Headquarters: New Delhi) and is a Navratna CPSE under MoHUA.

Exam Fact: Dholera Special Investment Region (DSIR) is India's first greenfield smart industrial city under the Delhi–Mumbai Industrial Corridor.

#24 • Government Schemes & Initiatives

Union Home Minister Amit Shah launched the 'PM Family Caretaker' pilot project in Gandhinagar, Gujarat. Up to what age of a child (from pregnancy) are welfare benefits tracked and provided?

  • A. 18 years
  • B. 6 years
  • C. 14 years
  • D. 21 years

Answer: 18 years

Coverage Window: Tracks maternal and child health, nutrition, immunization, and education from pregnancy up to 18 years of age.

Exam Fact: Launched in Gandhinagar, Gujarat (Amit Shah's Lok Sabha constituency).

#25 • Environment, Parks & GI Tags

Regarding Borjuli Biodiversity Heritage Site (notified July 2026): (1) Located in Sonitpur district, Assam; (2) Protects Oryza rufipogon (wild ancestor of cultivated rice); (3) Notified under Section 37 of Biological Diversity Act, 2002 (not Section 21); (4) Supported by ICAR-NBPGR and Assam State Biodiversity Board. Which statements are correct?

  • A. 1 and 3 only
  • B. 1, 2, and 4 only (Notified under Section 37 of Biodiversity Act, 2002)
  • C. 2 and 3 only
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1, 2, and 4 only (Notified under Section 37 of Biodiversity Act, 2002)

Correction Note: Biodiversity Heritage Sites (BHS) are notified by State Governments under Section 37 of the Biological Diversity Act, 2002.

Exam Fact: Nallur Tamarind Grove in Bengaluru, Karnataka was India's first Biodiversity Heritage Site (2007).

#26 • Science, Space & Environment

Which material did IIT Kanpur researchers use to build eco-friendly underground wave barriers to mitigate seismic/ground vibrations?

  • A. Recycled rubber
  • B. Coir fiber
  • C. Bamboo
  • D. Fly ash

Answer: Bamboo

Innovation: Bamboo-based underground wave barriers developed by IIT Kanpur (established 1959).

Exam Fact: Bamboo offers high tensile strength, sustainability, and cost-effectiveness as a natural vibration-damping barrier.

#27 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

In September 2026, the Ministry of Ports, Shipping and Waterways notified four ports as India's first 'Mega Ports'. Which of the following is NOT one of those four Mega Ports?

  • A. Paradip Port & Mundra Port
  • B. Jawaharlal Nehru Port Authority (Nhava Sheva)
  • C. Deendayal Port (Kandla)
  • D. Visakhapatnam Port

Answer: Visakhapatnam Port

Four Notified Mega Ports: JNPA (Nhava Sheva), Deendayal Port (Kandla), Paradip Port, and Mundra Port.

Exam Fact: Mega Ports are defined by cargo handling capacity exceeding 300 million metric tonnes per annum (MMTPA).

#28 • Science, Space & Environment

Where did Defence Minister Rajnath Singh lay the foundation stone for a ₹472 crore T-72 and T-90 tank overhaul facility?

  • A. Avadi, Tamil Nadu
  • B. Jabalpur, Madhya Pradesh
  • C. Medak, Telangana
  • D. Dehradun, Uttarakhand

Answer: Jabalpur, Madhya Pradesh

Location: Jabalpur, Madhya Pradesh (home to Vehicle Factory Jabalpur and Gun Carriage Factory under Armoured Vehicles Nigam Limited).

Exam Fact: Enhances indigenous MRO (Maintenance, Repair, and Overhaul) readiness for the Indian Army's main battle tank fleet.

#29 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

Regarding the Taglang La railway station project: (1) Lies on the Bhanupali–Bilaspur–Manali–Leh railway line; (2) Line length is 489 km (not 289 km); (3) Station elevation is 5,256 meters; (4) Surpasses China's Tanggula station (5,068 m). Which statements are correct?

  • A. 2 and 3 only
  • B. 1 and 2 only
  • C. 1, 3, and 4 only (Railway line length is 489 km)
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1, 3, and 4 only (Railway line length is 489 km)

Correction Note: The Bhanupali–Bilaspur–Manali–Leh strategic railway line is 489 km long (not 289 km).

Exam Fact: Provides all-weather high-speed rail connectivity to Ladakh.

#30 • Environment, Parks & GI Tags

Where did Cyclonic Storm 'Murasu'—the first North Indian Ocean cyclone given a Tamil name—make landfall?

  • A. Digha, West Bengal
  • B. Nagapattinam, Tamil Nadu
  • C. Gopalpur, Odisha
  • D. Ongole, Andhra Pradesh

Answer: Ongole, Andhra Pradesh

Name & Landfall: 'Murasu' (a traditional Tamil percussion drum) made landfall near Ongole in Prakasam district, Andhra Pradesh.

Exam Fact: Tropical cyclones in the North Indian Ocean are named sequentially by 13 WMO/ESCAP member countries.

#31 • Science, Space & Environment

Which two mobile apps did TRAI (established under the TRAI Act, 1997) integrate into its unified 'Network Anubhav' crowdsourced drive-test platform?

  • A. DND 3.0 and Sanchar Saathi
  • B. MySpeed and MyCall
  • C. Tarang Sanchar and KYM
  • D. UMANG and DigiLocker

Answer: MySpeed and MyCall

Apps Integrated: TRAI MySpeed (data speed testing) and TRAI MyCall (voice call quality rating).

Exam Fact: TRAI was established in 1997 (Headquarters: New Delhi).

#32 • Economy & Agriculture

Jupiter Wagons partnered with Lucchini RS and Sidenor/Simes to build a railway wheelset manufacturing plant in India. Lucchini RS belongs to which country?

  • A. Spain
  • B. Germany
  • C. Italy
  • D. France

Answer: Italy

Country: Italy (Lucchini RS is a leading Italian manufacturer of high-speed railway wheels and axles).

Exam Fact: Supports 'Make in India' self-reliance for Vande Bharat and Indian Railways rolling stock.

#33 • National & State Affairs

Regarding MoRTH's Local Toll Pass rules: (1) Covers non-commercial vehicles within 20 km of a fee plaza; (2) Annual fee outside municipal limits is ₹1,500; (3) Annual fee within municipal limits is ₹3,000; (4) Commercial vehicles are NOT eligible. Which statements are correct?

  • A. All 1, 2, 3, and 4
  • B. 1 and 4 only
  • C. 2 and 4 only
  • D. 1, 2, and 3 only (Commercial vehicles are excluded)

Answer: 1, 2, and 3 only (Commercial vehicles are excluded)

Correction Note: Only private (non-commercial) vehicles registered locally within 20 km are eligible; commercial vehicles are excluded.

Exam Fact: Annual pass fee is ₹1,500 outside municipal limits and ₹3,000 within municipal limits.

#34 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

Vizhinjam International Seaport (Kerala) received its first direct import container vessel from which international port?

  • A. Port of Singapore
  • B. Shanghai Port (China)
  • C. Ningbo Port (China)
  • D. Jebel Ali Port (UAE)

Answer: Ningbo Port (China)

Port Details: Ningbo-Zhoushan Port (China) to Vizhinjam International Seaport (Thiruvananthapuram, Kerala).

Exam Fact: Vizhinjam is India's first automated deep-water container transshipment port.

#35 • Economy & Agriculture

Union Minister Shivraj Singh Chouhan launched the 'Makhana Gaon Bachao Abhiyan' in June 2026 at Ramsiya village in which district of Madhya Pradesh?

  • A. Hoshangabad (Narmadapuram)
  • B. Vidisha
  • C. Sehore
  • D. Raisen

Answer: Raisen

Location: Ramsiya village, Raisen district, Madhya Pradesh.

Exam Fact: Shivraj Singh Chouhan is the Union Minister of Agriculture & Farmers' Welfare and Rural Development.

#36 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

In October 2026, the Union Cabinet approved a ₹5,000 crore commitment for which fund to support direct equity investment in MSMEs?

  • A. Self-Reliant India (SRI) Fund
  • B. SME Growth Fund (SGF)
  • C. Fund of Funds for Startups (FFS)
  • D. Credit Guarantee Fund Trust (CGTMSE)

Answer: SME Growth Fund (SGF)

Commitment: ₹5,000 crore equity infusion support via the SME Growth Fund (SGF).

Exam Fact: Helps high-potential MSMEs scale up and list on SME stock exchange platforms.

#37 • Infrastructure, Ports & Energy

Regarding the National Capital Region Planning Board (NCRPB): (1) Greenfield Namo Bharat transit-oriented cities planned; (2) Regional Plan 2041 framework; (3) Estimated cost is ₹5,000 crore (not ₹50,000 crore); (4) Chaired by Union Minister Manohar Lal Khattar covering Delhi, Haryana, UP, and Rajasthan. Which statements are correct?

  • A. 2 and 3 only
  • B. 1 and 3 only
  • C. 1, 2, and 4 only (Estimated cost is ₹5,000 crore)
  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

Answer: 1, 2, and 4 only (Estimated cost is ₹5,000 crore)

Correction Note: Estimated cost is ₹5,000 crore (not ₹50,000 crore).

Exam Fact: NCRPB is chaired by the Union Minister of Housing and Urban Affairs (Manohar Lal Khattar).

#38 • Economy & Agriculture

Under the VISTAR statistical modernization scheme, the number of major national surveys has been expanded from 43 to how many?

  • A. 60
  • B. 48
  • C. 55
  • D. 51

Answer: 51

Expansion: Increased from 43 surveys to 51 major surveys (including Periodic Labour Force Survey).

Exam Fact: Enhances high-frequency district- and state-level economic data.

#39 • Polity, Bills & Governance

Regarding Central Motor Vehicles Rules (CMVR), 1989 amendments (October 2026): (1) Certifies E20/E85 flex-fuel vehicles; (2) Covers B100 biodiesel, hydrogen, and CNG vehicles; (3) Gross vehicle weight limit is 3,000–3,500 kg (not 3,500–4,000 kg). Which statements are correct?

  • A. 1 and 3 only
  • B. 1 and 2 only (Weight category limit is 3,000–3,500 kg)
  • C. 2 and 3 only
  • D. All 1, 2, and 3

Answer: 1 and 2 only (Weight category limit is 3,000–3,500 kg)

Correction Note: The specified vehicle weight threshold is 3,000 kg to 3,500 kg (not 3,500–4,000 kg).

Exam Fact: Promotes clean alternative fuels and flex-fuel adoption across India.

#40 • Government Schemes & Initiatives

On which date was the PM Vishwakarma Yojana (which completed 3 years in 2026 with over 30 lakh registered artisans) launched?

  • A. 17 September 2023
  • B. 15 August 2023
  • C. 2 October 2023
  • D. 1 January 2024

Answer: 17 September 2023

Launch Date: 17 September 2023 (Vishwakarma Jayanti) | Nodal Ministry: Ministry of MSME.

Exam Fact: Covers 18 traditional trades and offers collateral-free enterprise loans up to ₹3 lakh at 5% concessional interest.

#41 • Science, Space & Environment

Where is the AI-powered supercomputing facility 'PARAM Pragya' (inaugurated by PM Modi during IIT Delhi's 57th Convocation) located?

  • A. Jhajjar Campus, Haryana
  • B. Hauz Khas Campus, New Delhi
  • C. Sonipat Campus, Haryana
  • D. Noida Campus, Uttar Pradesh

Answer: Sonipat Campus, Haryana

Location: IIT Delhi's Sonipat Campus in Haryana.

Exam Fact: Deployed under the National Supercomputing Mission (NSM) jointly steered by MeitY and DST.

#42 • Economy & Agriculture

APEDA flagged off a trial export of 1.5 tonnes of fresh organic kiwis from Ziro Valley (Arunachal Pradesh) to which international city?

  • A. Doha, Qatar
  • B. London, UK
  • C. Singapore
  • D. Dubai, UAE

Answer: Dubai, UAE

Origin & Destination: Ziro Valley (Lower Subansiri district, Arunachal Pradesh) to Dubai, UAE.

Exam Fact: Arunachal Pradesh was the first Indian state to obtain organic certification for kiwis under Mission Organic Value Chain Development.

#43 • National & State Affairs

Regarding Centres of Excellence for Classical Languages (Ministry of Education): (1) Approved for Assamese, Bengali, Marathi, Pali, and Prakrit; (2) Assamese Centre is at Tezpur University (not Gauhati University); (3) Bengali Centre is at Visva-Bharati University. Which statements are correct?

  • A. 2 and 3 only
  • B. 1 and 2 only
  • C. 1 and 3 only (Assamese Centre is at Tezpur University)
  • D. All 1, 2, and 3

Answer: 1, 3, and 4 only (Headquartered in New Delhi)

What is the maximum annual remittance limit that a resident individual can make per financial year under the RBI's Liberalised Remittance Scheme (LRS)?

  • C. USD 2,50,000 ($250,000)
  • D. USD 10,00,000 ($1,000,000)

#5 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

Under RBI's revised bulk deposit norms (₹3 crore and above), differential interest rates on bulk term deposits are aligned with which liquidity metric?

  • A. LCR (Liquidity Coverage Ratio) run-off rates
  • B. Statutory Liquidity Ratio (SLR) haircuts
  • C. Cash Reserve Ratio (CRR) fortnightly averages
  • D. Net Stable Funding Ratio (NSFR) weights

Answer: LCR (Liquidity Coverage Ratio) run-off rates

Metric: Liquidity Coverage Ratio (LCR) run-off rates (bulk deposits are defined as single rupee term deposits of ₹3 crore and above for Scheduled Commercial Banks).

Exam Fact: LCR requires banks to hold sufficient High-Quality Liquid Assets (HQLA) to survive a 30-day acute liquidity stress scenario.

#10 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

  • B. 50%
  • C. 75%
  • D. 100%

Answer: 75%

Risk Weight Rule: 75% of the ECLGS 5.0 guaranteed credit exposure attracts 0% risk weight (managed via NCGTC).

Exam Fact: Lower risk weights reduce the Tier-1/Tier-2 capital banks must set aside under Basel III CRAR norms.

#27 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

Under which NBFC regulatory layer is Tata Sons classified by the RBI (whose plea for exemption/deregistration was rejected by RBI)?

  • B. Top Layer NBFC (NBFC-TL)
  • C. Middle Layer NBFC (NBFC-ML)
  • D. Upper Layer NBFC (NBFC-UL)

Answer: Upper Layer NBFC (NBFC-UL)

Exam Fact: NBFC-UL entities are subject to bank-like prudential regulations and mandatory stock exchange listing within 3 years of identification.

#37 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

What is the primary statutory mandate of the RBI's Monetary Policy Committee (MPC)?

  • A. Auditing state government budgets
  • B. Fixing commercial bank employee salaries
  • C. Approving foreign direct investment in defence
  • D. Maintaining price stability (inflation target of 4% ± 2%) while keeping in mind the objective of growth

Answer: Maintaining price stability (inflation target of 4% ± 2%) while keeping in mind the objective of growth

Flexible Inflation Targeting (FIT): CPI inflation target of 4% with a tolerance band of ±2% (i.e., 2% to 6%).

Exam Fact: If average CPI inflation stays outside 2%–6% for 3 consecutive quarters, RBI must submit a written report to the Central Government.

#38 • Banking, Finance & Regulatory

In September 2026, RBI announced an Open Market Operation (OMO) purchase auction worth ₹1,00,000 crore primarily to achieve which objective?

  • A. Managing durable systemic liquidity (absorbing/injecting liquidity)
  • B. Raising equity capital for public sector banks
  • C. Setting retail gold loan interest ceilings
  • D. Privatizing regional rural banks

Answer: Managing durable systemic liquidity (absorbing/injecting liquidity)

Monetary Tool: Open Market Operations (OMOs) involve outright purchase or sale of Government Securities (G-Secs) by RBI to regulate durable money supply.

Exam Fact: OMO purchases inject rupee liquidity into the banking system; OMO sales absorb surplus liquidity.

  • D. All 1, 2, 3, and 4

  • B. 1 and 2 only (Permafrost stores 1,700 billion tonnes of carbon)
  • C. 2 and 3 only
  • D. All 1, 2, and 3

Answer: 1 and 2 only (Permafrost stores 1,700 billion tonnes of carbon)

Repeated From Previous Sources: Veranda Part 6 Q.871 & Q.880 — State of the Cryosphere Report (Arctic 4x warming & 1,700B tonnes permafrost carbon).

Exam Fact: Cryosphere encompasses all frozen water on Earth—glaciers, ice sheets, sea ice, snow cover, and permafrost.
